## PACKAGE A

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

DELIVERABLE
One 9:16 vertical UGC-style performance video, ~25 seconds, single continuous creator narrative across 4 cuts, captioned, feed/reels-ready (Instagram/YouTube Shorts/Meta Ads placement), for a fictional Indian D2C skincare brand.

OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION
The customer wants a believable, low-polish creator testimonial (not a TVC) that drives purchase intent for a fragrance-free 2% salicylic-acid serum aimed at oily, blemish-prone adult skin. Success = viewer perceives one real woman genuinely explaining why this product earned a place in her routine, retains attention through a fast hook, and walks away with exactly one clear, non-medical purchase reason. Compliance constraint is central: no invented efficacy %, no "clinically proven," no dermatologist endorsement, no cure/treatment language — only descriptive, honest cosmetic framing (oil control feel, lightweight texture, fragrance-free, ingredient percentage as a factual formulation detail, not a claim of outcome).

CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA
"The serum I reach for on my oily days" — a relatable, slightly imperfect, phone-shot confession from a woman who tried multiple products, found this one comfortable to use daily, and states one honest personal reason she keeps repurchasing it: it controls shine without stinging or drying her skin out. No lab talk, no doctor, no before/after miracle — just a credible daily-use moment.

MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY
1. Primary (must land): One clear purchase reason — "controls oil without drying/irritating skin, fragrance-free, comfortable daily use."
2. Secondary: Product identity — brand name, product name, key formulation fact (2% salicylic acid, fragrance-free) shown as on-screen text, not spoken as a clinical claim.
3. Tertiary: CTA — soft, platform-native ("link in bio" / "available online now"), no named external URL.
Hierarchy is delivered in this order across the 25s: Hook (relatable problem) → Credibility beat (what she tried before / what changed) → Demo (visual proof of texture/use) → Single reason + CTA.

VISUAL_SYSTEM
- Aspect ratio: 9:16, full-bleed vertical, shot as if on a smartphone front camera + one cutaway on rear camera for product macro.
- Look: natural handheld/selfie framing, slight handshake, real bathroom/bedroom daylight (soft window light, no studio grade), skin shown with visible natural texture/pores — explicitly NOT airbrushed or beauty-filtered.
- Wardrobe: casual home clothing (plain tee/kurti), minimal/no makeup, hair loosely tied — signals authenticity.
- Color: warm-neutral natural skin tones; brand accent color (sage-green/off-white, per packaging) appears only via product and on-screen text chips, never color-grading the whole frame.
- Captions: bold sans-serif burned-in captions, word-synced, bottom-safe zone, brand accent color for 2–3 key words per line (e.g., "fragrance-free," "2% salicylic acid," "oil control").
- No stock-ad transitions, no slow-mo glam shots, no glossy studio product tabletop — keep product shot handheld/macro on real skin/hand.

PRODUCTION_RECIPE
Format: 4 shots, straight cuts (no dissolves), total ~25s, vertical 9:16, 24–30fps handheld aesthetic.
Casting: one Indian woman, mid-20s–early-30s, natural presentation, conversational Hinglish-leaning English delivery, warm/direct eye contact with lens.
Fictional brand: "Clarra Skin" — Product: "Clarra 2% Salicylic Acid Serum" (fragrance-free), simple minimal white/sage pump bottle with visible small label reading "Fragrance-Free · 2% Salicylic Acid" (fictional, generic pharmacy-style label, no real regulatory marks/certifications invented).

SHOT LIST

Shot 1 — HOOK (0:00–0:03, ~3s)
Purpose: Stop scroll, establish relatability.
Framing: Close-up selfie-style, face + shoulders, slight handheld sway, natural window light.
Action/VO: She looks straight into lens, mid-sentence energy: "If you have oily, acne-prone skin like me... you need to hear this."
On-screen text: none yet (let hook breathe).

Shot 2 — PROBLEM/CREDIBILITY (0:03–0:09, ~6s)
Purpose: Build trust via honest past-experience framing (no stats).
Framing: Same selfie angle, she picks up the Clarra bottle from a shelf/counter into frame, turns it slightly.
Action/VO: "I've tried so many serums — some broke me out more, some just dried my skin out completely. This is the one I actually stuck with."
On-screen text (appears late in shot): "Clarra 2% Salicylic Acid Serum" (small, lower third).

Shot 3 — DEMO (0:09–0:18, ~9s)
Purpose: Natural product demonstration, tactile proof.
Framing: Slight angle change (rear-camera macro cut) — hand dispenses one pump onto fingertip, then cuts back to selfie as she pats it onto cheek/jaw, visible sheen/absorption.
Action/VO: "It's fragrance-free, super lightweight, and it actually sinks in fast — no sticky, no stinging." (she pats product in, natural facial reaction — comfortable, casual smile)
On-screen text: "Fragrance-Free" then "Lightweight Gel-Serum" (sequential caption chips).

Shot 4 — SINGLE REASON + CTA (0:18–0:25, ~7s)
Purpose: Deliver the one clear purchase reason and close.
Framing: Back to tight selfie close-up, direct eye contact, calmer pace.
Action/VO: "Honestly, it's the only one that controls my oil without leaving my skin tight or irritated — that's why it's stayed in my routine." Beat. "Link in my bio if you want to try it."
On-screen text: Brand logo lockup "Clarra Skin" + CTA text "Available Online Now" (no URL shown).

GENERATION_PROMPTS

Shot 1 prompt:
"Vertical 9:16 handheld smartphone selfie video, close-up of a natural-looking Indian woman in her mid-to-late 20s, minimal makeup, casual home clothing, soft warm daylight from a window, slightly imperfect handheld framing, talking directly and energetically into the camera lens, mouth mid-speech, authentic UGC creator aesthetic, visible natural skin texture, no beauty filter, no studio lighting, realistic bathroom/bedroom background softly out of focus, 3 seconds, casual confident expression."

Shot 2 prompt:
"Vertical 9:16 handheld smartphone selfie video continuing from previous shot, same Indian woman, now picking up a small minimalist white and sage-green pump bottle labeled 'Clarra 2% Salicylic Acid Serum, Fragrance-Free' from a bathroom shelf, turning it slightly toward camera while continuing to speak, natural warm window light, authentic slightly shaky handheld UGC style, real skin texture, casual tone, 6 seconds."

Shot 2b (macro insert) prompt:
"Vertical 9:16 close-up macro shot, hand dispensing a single pump of clear lightweight gel-serum from a minimalist white and sage-green bottle onto a fingertip, natural daylight, shallow depth of field, realistic skin and product texture, UGC phone-camera macro aesthetic, no studio gloss, 2 seconds."

Shot 3 prompt:
"Vertical 9:16 handheld selfie video, same Indian woman patting a lightweight clear serum onto her cheek and jaw with fingertips, natural relaxed expression, slight sheen of product absorbing into skin, soft window daylight, authentic UGC creator demo style, visible real skin texture and pores, no retouching, casual home background, 9 seconds."

Shot 4 prompt:
"Vertical 9:16 handheld selfie video, same Indian woman now closer to camera, calm confident direct eye contact, speaking her final line with a warm genuine smile at the end, natural daylight, authentic UGC aesthetic, real skin texture, casual home setting softly blurred behind her, 7 seconds."

DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS
- Burned-in captions (word-synced, brand-accent color highlights) — added in post, not generated.
- On-screen text chips: "Clarra 2% Salicylic Acid Serum," "Fragrance-Free," "Lightweight Gel-Serum" — typeset overlays, not part of generated footage.
- End-card lockup: Clarra Skin logo + "Available Online Now" — static graphic overlay, fixed brand asset, composited in edit.
- Product label text on bottle must be locked as a supplied reference asset (fictional label artwork) to keep spelling/formulation text consistent across shots 2–4, rather than left to per-shot generation.

AUDIO_AND_EDIT
- Dialogue (single continuous VO/lip-sync across 4 cuts, ~25s total, natural conversational Hinglish-inflected English pace):
"If you have oily, acne-prone skin like me... you need to hear this. I've tried so many serums — some broke me out more, some just dried my skin out completely. This is the one I actually stuck with. It's fragrance-free, super lightweight, and it actually sinks in fast — no sticky, no stinging. Honestly, it's the only one that controls my oil without leaving my skin tight or irritated — that's why it's stayed in my routine. Link in my bio if you want to try it."
- Music: low-volume neutral lo-fi/acoustic trending-style bed under VO, ducked significantly beneath voice, fades in at 0:00, fades out at 0:25 — must not overpower speech (this is a talking-head ad, not a music video).
- SFX: soft bottle-cap/pump click at Shot 2b for tactile realism.
- Edit pacing: 4 hard cuts, no transitions/wipes, slight jump-cut energy consistent with authentic creator editing (not glossy match-cuts).
- Captions burned in throughout, synced to VO, mandatory for sound-off viewing (performance-ad best practice).

FAILURE_PREVENTION
- Guard against generated on-screen text errors: lock brand/product name and ingredient text as a fixed overlay asset applied in post, not left to model-generated in-frame text.
- Guard against face/identity drift across the 4 shots: use one locked character reference (same actress features, wardrobe, hairstyle) across all prompts; regenerate any shot where facial identity, skin tone, or hairstyle shifts noticeably.
- Guard against over-polished/AI-glossy skin rendering: explicitly reinforce "visible natural skin texture, no beauty filter, no studio gloss" in every prompt; reject outputs with airbrushed/plastic skin.
- Guard against product bottle inconsistency: lock bottle shape/color/label as a fixed reference asset across shots 2, 2b, and any product visibility in shot 3–4.
- Guard against claim drift: script explicitly avoids "clinically proven," "dermatologist recommended," percentage efficacy, cure/treatment language, or guarantees; only factual formulation descriptors (fragrance-free, 2% salicylic acid, lightweight) and first-person subjective experience are used. Any generated ad-lib dialogue must be checked against this script and re-recorded/regenerated if it drifts into medical or statistical claims.
- Guard against multiple-person or background-person intrusion: prompts specify single subject, softly blurred empty background.
- Guard against wrong aspect ratio or CTA referencing a real/external URL: enforce 9:16 output and "link in bio" phrasing only, no spoken or on-screen URL/domain.

HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK
- 9:16 vertical: yes, specified throughout.
- One believable Indian woman, direct to camera: yes, single actress, all 4 shots.
- Natural product demonstration: yes, Shots 2–3.
- One clear purchase reason communicated: yes, explicit single reason in Shot 4 ("controls oil without leaving skin tight or irritated").
- Feels like creator content, not TVC: yes — handheld, natural light, no studio gloss, casual wardrobe, jump-cut editing.
- Visually controlled/commercially usable: yes — locked brand assets, consistent character/product reference, clean captioning system.
- No invented clinical statistics, guarantees, or medical claims: yes — script audited, only descriptive/subjective, non-medical language used.
- ~25 seconds target: yes — 3+6+9+7 = 25s.
- No external website: yes — CTA uses "link in bio" / "available online," no URL named or shown.

## PACKAGE B

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

DELIVERABLE
One 9:16 vertical video, ~25 seconds, single continuous creator-style ad ("scroll-stopper" UGC ad) for a fictional Indian D2C skincare brand, featuring one Indian woman speaking to camera, demonstrating the product, and delivering one clear purchase reason. Final format: 1080×1920, 24–30fps, burned-in captions, platform-ready (Instagram Reels/YouTube Shorts style), no website/URL shown.

OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION
The brief calls for performance advertising that mimics authentic creator content rather than a studio commercial. Success = viewer believes a real person is recommending a real product, retains attention through a natural hook–problem–demo–payoff–CTA arc, and remembers exactly one reason to buy. Constraints require restraint: no fabricated clinical data, no medical/guarantee language, no external site, natural (not aspirational-glam) visual treatment.

CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA
A relatable young Indian woman films herself in her bedroom/bathroom, mid-skincare-routine, and tells the camera the one thing that made her switch to this serum: it treats oily, breakout-prone skin without the strong smell or heavy/sticky feel she hated in other salicylic acid serums. The entire video is built around this single sensory-and-lifestyle purchase reason — "fragrance-free, lightweight, sinks in fast" — never a clinical percentage-improvement or medical promise.

MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY
1. Hook (attention): "If you have oily, breakout-prone skin, listen to this."
2. Relatable problem: other serums smell strong or feel greasy by afternoon.
3. Product demo (proof): shows real application, quick absorption.
4. Single purchase reason (core payoff): fragrance-free + lightweight + non-sticky = why she keeps repurchasing.
5. Brand recall + soft CTA: product name spoken + on-screen text, "link in bio" (no URL).
No statistics, no "clinically proven," no "cures acne," no before/after claim.

VISUAL_SYSTEM
- Aesthetic: authentic phone-shot UGC — handheld micro-shake, natural window light, no cinematic grade, no beauty filter, visible ordinary bedroom/bathroom.
- Talent: Indian woman, mid-20s–early 30s, warm brown skin tone, natural skin texture (slight visible shine/oiliness consistent with target skin type, not exaggerated or "flawless"), minimal makeup, casual wardrobe (oversized tee or home kurta).
- Product: matte white/sage-green dropper bottle, minimal typography label reading "Rooh Clarity Serum — 2% Salicylic Acid, Fragrance-Free," simple modern logo mark.
- Framing: vertical selfie-style compositions (arm's-length or propped phone), occasional close-up insert for demo texture.
- Color: natural, slightly warm, unfiltered — consistent with real creator footage, not commercial gloss.
- On-screen text: lightweight caption style matching native platform captions (not brand-heavy motion graphics), plus one small end-card text with brand name.

PRODUCTION_RECIPE
Format: 9:16, 1080×1920, 24–30fps, ~25s total, 5 shots, jump-cut style edit.
Location: home bedroom or bathroom with natural daylight (window light, no studio lighting rig look).
Camera: simulated phone camera (selfie framing + one handheld demo insert), slight natural shake, no gimbal-smoothness.
Wardrobe/Props: casual homewear, the serum bottle, no other product clutter.
Talent direction: conversational pacing, natural pauses, genuine smile, no scripted-sounding delivery — treat as if talking to a friend.
Continuity: same actress, wardrobe, location, lighting and bottle design across all 5 shots.

GENERATION_PROMPTS
Shot 1 — HOOK (0:00–0:03, 3s)
Prompt: "Vertical 9:16 phone-style selfie video. A young Indian woman, mid-20s, warm brown skin, natural everyday makeup, wearing a casual oversized t-shirt, sits in a softly lit bedroom near a window with natural daylight. Handheld phone framing, slight natural camera shake, close-medium shot, direct eye contact with lens. She holds a matte sage-green serum bottle near her collarbone and says with a casual, slightly urgent tone: 'Okay if you have oily, breakout-prone skin, listen to this.' Authentic creator-content look, no cinematic color grade, no beauty filter."

Shot 2 — PROBLEM (0:03–0:09, 6s)
Prompt: "Continuation of same woman, same room, same lighting and wardrobe, medium selfie framing, natural handheld motion. She speaks candidly with mild hand gestures, slightly furrowed brow of relatability: 'I've tried so many salicylic acid serums but they either smell so strong or leave my face feeling greasy by noon.' Natural skin texture visible, unfiltered, authentic UGC tone."

Shot 3 — DEMO (0:09–0:16, 7s)
Prompt: "Same woman, same setting, camera shifts to a closer demo angle at chest/face height. She dispenses two drops of a light serum from a dropper bottle onto her fingertip, then pats it gently onto her cheek and jawline in front of a mirror or window light. Include one brief macro insert (1–2 seconds) of the clear lightweight serum texture absorbing into skin. Natural sound of tapping/patting. She says: 'This one's fragrance-free, just two pumps... and it literally sinks in in like ten seconds, no residue.' Real, unpolished demonstration, no gloss VFX."

Shot 4 — PURCHASE REASON PAYOFF (0:16–0:22, 6s)
Prompt: "Same woman returns to direct-to-camera medium selfie framing, same lighting/location, holding the serum bottle at chest level with a satisfied, genuine smile. She says: 'That's literally why I keep repurchasing it — it calms my breakouts without the heavy serum smell or sticky feel.' Natural delivery, authentic pacing, no exaggerated expression."

Shot 5 — CTA / PACKSHOT (0:22–0:25, 3s)
Prompt: "Close-up shot of the same matte sage-green serum bottle held up to camera by the woman, her face softly out of focus in background, natural window light. Label clearly readable: 'Rooh Clarity Serum — 2% Salicylic Acid, Fragrance-Free.' She says: 'It's called Rooh Clarity Serum, go check it out.' Small on-screen text overlay appears: 'Rooh Clarity Serum' and 'Link in bio' (no URL shown). Authentic phone-camera finish, no polished commercial lighting."

DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS
- Product label text and logo: fixed asset, applied identically in every shot (not left to per-shot regeneration) to avoid brand/label drift.
- Burned-in captions: added in post-production, synced to actual spoken lines, matching platform-native caption styling.
- End-card text ("Rooh Clarity Serum" / "Link in bio"): inserted as a deterministic text overlay, not generated imagery, to guarantee accurate spelling and no accidental URL/claim insertion.
- Ingredient/percentage claim ("2% Salicylic Acid, Fragrance-Free") is treated as fixed label copy, never spoken as a "clinically proven" or performance statistic.

AUDIO_AND_EDIT
- Voice: natural conversational Indian-English/Hinglish-inflected delivery, same actress throughout, synced lip movement to dialogue.
- Music: optional low-volume trending-style ambient beat under dialogue (not a swelling commercial score), kept subordinate to voice.
- SFX: natural room tone, subtle product/tapping sound during demo.
- Edit style: quick jump cuts between the 5 shots (no dissolves/wipes), consistent with authentic creator editing; minimal color grading (light warmth only, no LUT-heavy commercial polish).
- Captions: burned in, word-synced, standard UGC caption style.

FAILURE_PREVENTION
- Risk: overly glossy/AI-perfect look reading as "TVC not UGC" → mitigated via handheld shake, natural light only, unfiltered skin texture instruction in every prompt.
- Risk: actress inconsistency across shots (face/wardrobe/location drift) → mitigated by repeating identical talent/location/lighting/wardrobe description in every shot prompt.
- Risk: invented clinical claims ("clinically proven," "reduces acne by X%") → explicitly excluded from all scripted lines; only sensory/lifestyle language used.
- Risk: medical/guarantee language ("cures," "eliminates acne") → avoided; language limited to "calms breakouts," a non-medical, experiential phrase.
- Risk: accidental display of a website URL → CTA scripted as "link in bio" with no domain text anywhere in overlay or dialogue.
- Risk: label illegibility/brand drift → label design and text treated as a fixed deterministic asset applied identically across shots.
- Risk: mismatched lip sync or unnatural pacing → dialogue kept short, conversational, with natural pauses built into each shot's script.

HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK
- 9:16 vertical format: satisfied.
- Performance-oriented UGC style, not polished TVC: satisfied via handheld/natural-light/unfiltered treatment.
- Fictional Indian D2C skincare brand: satisfied ("Rooh Clarity Serum").
- Fragrance-free 2% salicylic-acid facial serum, adults with oily/blemish-prone skin: satisfied in label copy and script framing.
- One believable Indian woman speaking directly to camera: satisfied, single consistent talent across all shots.
- Natural demonstration of product: satisfied in Shot 3.
- One clear purchase reason communicated: satisfied — fragrance-free/lightweight/non-sticky feel (Shot 4 payoff), no competing secondary claims.
- Credible creator content, visually controlled, commercially usable: satisfied via consistent talent/location/lighting/product design plus deterministic label/caption handling.
- No invented clinical statistics, guarantees, or medical claims: satisfied — script audited, contains no numeric efficacy claims or medical language.
- ~25 seconds total duration: satisfied (3+6+7+6+3 = 25s).
- No external website: satisfied — CTA uses "link in bio," no URL text or spoken domain anywhere.

## PACKAGE C

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

DELIVERABLE
One (1) vertical performance video, 9:16, 1080×1920, ~24–26 seconds, MP4, burned-in captions, silent-autoplay-safe, optimized for Instagram Reels/Stories, YouTube Shorts and Meta Feed placements. Master delivered with captions-on (primary) and a caption-off version as a secondary cutdown asset.

OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION
Produce a low-fidelity, high-credibility creator-style ad for a fictional Indian D2C brand's fragrance-free 2% salicylic-acid serum, targeted at adults with oily, blemish-prone skin. The video must function as a scroll-stopping performance asset (fast hook, visible demo, one clear purchase reason, soft CTA) while staying strictly within honest, non-clinical claim territory — no invented statistics, no "clears acne," no "dermatologist proven," no guarantees. Success = looks like an unpaid, unscripted phone-shot recommendation, not a studio TVC.

CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA
"The No-Sticky-Layer Test" — a woman filming herself at her bathroom mirror mid-routine reacts, in real time, to the one thing that made her stick with this serum: it's fragrance-free and it sinks in fast without the greasy/sticky film she's used to from other actives on oily skin. She ends by holding up her own visibly half-used bottle — a small authenticity cue that signals repeat purchase without stating any claim or number.

MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY
1. Primary purchase reason (spoken + on-screen): fragrance-free, absorbs fast, no sticky/greasy layer — comfortable for daily use on oily/blemish-prone skin.
2. Product identity: brand name + "2% Salicylic Acid Serum" (factual formulation detail, not a clinical claim).
3. Trust cue: personal repurchase behavior ("this is what I keep repurchasing"), not a guarantee or statistic.
4. Soft CTA: availability cue only, no URL, no external website reference.

VISUAL_SYSTEM
- Framing: vertical handheld selfie-style, slight natural hand-shake, phone-camera field of view, occasional soft focus breathing (not gimbal-smooth).
- Lighting: single soft window light + practical bathroom light, warm-neutral, slightly overexposed highlights typical of phone front camera — no studio lighting rig look.
- Color: minimal grading, slightly desaturated warm skin tones, natural contrast — "iPhone memo" look, not a commercial LUT.
- Talent: Indian woman, late 20s, no heavy makeup, hair loosely tied, casual home tee/robe, subtle natural shine/texture on skin (relatable, not exaggerated or stigmatized).
- Set: home bathroom mirror or bedroom vanity with believable Indian-home clutter (towel, small plant, everyday bottles) — not a styled set.
- Typography: bold rounded sans-serif captions, white text/black soft outline, one accent-color keyword highlight box per line (matches bottle accent color), positioned lower-middle third to survive UI crop.
- Product prop: single consistent serum bottle — minimal dropper bottle, brand wordmark "Purenva," small label reading "2% Salicylic Acid Serum · Fragrance-Free" — same physical prop/reference used in every shot, shown visibly half-used in the final shot.

PRODUCTION_RECIPE
1. Lock two reference assets before any shot generation: (a) a single consistent face/identity reference of the actress, (b) a single consistent product bottle/label reference image. Feed both references into every subsequent shot generation to guarantee continuity of face and packaging across cuts.
2. Generate/shoot the six shots below in sequence at native 9:16, then edit as quick jump-cuts (typical UGC pacing) rather than smooth transitions — mismatched micro-jumps between shots reinforce authenticity.
3. Record/generate dialogue as native sync audio per shot (not a separate VO layer) to preserve lip-sync realism.
4. Add captions, keyword highlight boxes, and end-card text in post as deterministic overlays (not part of generation) for accuracy control.
5. Light color pass only: reduce saturation ~10%, keep exposure phone-realistic, no beauty smoothing.

GENERATION_PROMPTS

Shot 1 — HOOK (0:00–0:03, 3s)
Prompt: "9:16 vertical, handheld selfie-style phone video. Indian woman, late 20s, casual home tee, hair tied back, natural everyday skin with subtle oily shine, standing at a home bathroom mirror in soft morning window light. She looks directly into the lens with a slightly exasperated, relatable expression, holding the phone at arm's length, mild natural hand-shake. She speaks: 'Okay if you have oily, acne-prone skin like me — you need to hear this.' Naturalistic phone-camera lighting, no studio look, no makeup, authentic amateur framing with slight headroom cropping typical of selfie video."

Shot 2 — PRODUCT INTRO (0:03–0:07, 4s)
Prompt: "Same woman and setting, continuous handheld selfie framing, now holding up [reference product bottle] toward the camera at chest height, label facing lens, natural hand movement, mirror bathroom background softly visible behind. She says: 'This is Purenva's 2% salicylic acid serum. It's completely fragrance-free.' Lighting and identity locked to reference face and reference bottle. Slight phone-camera motion blur on hand movement, casual not staged."

Shot 3 — DISPENSE CLOSE-UP (0:07–0:11, 4s)
Prompt: "Close-up handheld shot, phone camera angled down toward her hand, shallow bathroom counter background out of focus. She squeezes the dropper of [reference product bottle] and releases two drops onto her fingertip, natural unsteady close-up framing. She says only: 'Just two drops—' Natural window light, visible dropper mechanism, authentic texture of liquid serum, no glossy commercial macro lighting."

Shot 4 — APPLICATION (0:11–0:16, 5s)
Prompt: "Return to selfie framing at arm's length, same woman, same bathroom mirror setting. She presses the serum into her cheeks and forehead with fingertips using light patting motions (not rubbing), visible natural absorption on skin. She says: '—and I press it in, not rub. Look how fast that's absorbing.' Realistic skin sheen reducing slightly as product absorbs, natural facial micro-expressions, handheld continuous shake."

Shot 5 — ABSORPTION/TEXTURE REACTION (0:16–0:20, 4s)
Prompt: "Selfie framing, close-medium shot, same woman touching her cheek lightly with fingertips, checking texture, slightly surprised/pleased expression. She says: 'No sticky layer, no fragrance stinging my skin. Just sinks right in.' Same locked lighting and identity, natural finish with no shine exaggeration, authentic subtle skin texture retained (not airbrushed)."

Shot 6 — CTA + BOTTLE HOLD (0:20–0:25, 5s)
Prompt: "Selfie framing returning to arm's length, same woman smiling naturally, holding up the same [reference product bottle] now visibly about half-used (lower liquid level, slightly worn label edge) toward camera. She says: 'Honestly? That's the whole reason I keep repurchasing this one.' Soft warm natural light, casual sign-off energy, no scripted TV-ad polish, slight handheld settle as clip ends."

DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS
- Product label/wordmark design ("Purenva — 2% Salicylic Acid Serum · Fragrance-Free"): fixed static asset, designed once, locked as reference for all shots.
- Burned-in captions and keyword highlight boxes: added in post, exact text specified below, not left to model interpretation.
- End card (final 0.5–1s optional overlay within Shot 6): brand wordmark + "Fragrance-Free · 2% Salicylic Acid" static text card, no URL.
- Any small legal/compliance text (e.g., "Individual usage experience may vary") if required by internal review: added as static burned-in text, bottom-safe area, not generated.

Caption text per shot (verbatim, timed to dialogue):
1. "oily + acne-prone skin? read this 👇"
2. "PURENVA · 2% Salicylic Acid Serum"
3. "fragrance-free ✔"
4. "absorbs FAST, no rubbing needed"
5. "no sticky layer. no sting."
6. "why I keep repurchasing this 🔁"

AUDIO_AND_EDIT
- Sync dialogue recorded/generated natively per shot (real lip-sync, not overlaid VO).
- Light room tone under dialogue; no dramatic SFX.
- Low-volume trending-style minimal lo-fi/ambient background track (non-copyrighted/licensed), ducked well under voice.
- Edit rhythm: hard jump-cuts between all six shots, no crossfades, matching authentic single-continuous-take UGC feel.
- Runtime: 24–26 seconds total; export 1080×1920, 24–30fps, captions burned in with 2–3 word max per line, positioned to avoid platform UI overlap.

FAILURE_PREVENTION
- Identity drift risk: mitigated by locking one face reference across all six shots.
- Product inconsistency risk: mitigated by locking one bottle/label reference image across all six shots, including the "half-used" detail only appearing in Shot 6.
- Claim-creep risk: script and captions audited to ensure no percentages/results claims beyond the factual "2% salicylic acid, fragrance-free" formulation facts; no "clears," "cures," "guaranteed," "dermatologist-recommended" language used anywhere.
- Over-polish risk: explicit direction against studio lighting, gimbal smoothness, or beauty retouching in every prompt to preserve UGC credibility.
- Representation risk: skin shown with realistic, non-stigmatizing mild oiliness/texture — avoided exaggerated or prosthetic acne depiction.
- CTA risk: no website/link language used anywhere in script, captions, or end card, per constraint.
- Trademark risk: "Purenva" is a fictional name for this production; recommend a trademark clearance check before any real commercial deployment.

HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK
- 9:16 vertical: ✔ specified throughout.
- Fictional Indian D2C brand: ✔ "Purenva" fictional wordmark.
- Fragrance-free 2% salicylic-acid serum, oily/blemish-prone target: ✔ stated as factual formulation detail only.
- One believable Indian woman, direct to camera, natural demo: ✔ single talent, continuous selfie-style demo across all shots.
- One clear purchase reason: ✔ "fragrance-free, fast-absorbing, no sticky layer" is the sole reinforced reason.
- Credible creator content, not polished TVC: ✔ handheld, unstaged lighting, jump-cuts, no beauty grade.
- Visually controlled, commercially usable: ✔ locked identity/product references, deterministic caption system, clean legal posture.
- No invented clinical statistics/guarantees/medical claims: ✔ verified across script, captions, end card.
- ~25 seconds: ✔ shot durations sum to 25s.
- No external website: ✔ no URL, no "link in bio," no site reference anywhere in script, captions, or CTA.

# CONTEST V09 — B05

## PACKAGE A

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

DELIVERABLE
One continuous narrative video sequence, exactly 6 shots, hard cuts only, target runtime ≈ 40 seconds, 16:9, dialogue-driven two-hander café confrontation scene with synced ambient sound and restrained line delivery.

OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION
The customer wants a small, controlled dramatic scene — not a montage or a demo of camera technique. Success = the viewer instantly reads (a) who discovered what, (b) the emotional temperature (tense but contained, never shouted), and (c) the physical relationship between the two people at every moment, achieved through disciplined shot economy rather than coverage or movement.

CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA
Tension is carried by stillness, not motion. The camera never moves; the actors barely do. A fixed 180° axis and a single motivated light source turn a plain café table into a pressure chamber — the calm public setting amplifies the private rupture. One physical object (a folded letter, half-hidden under a hand) becomes the silent proof of concealment; the edit holds on it just long enough to register without over-explaining.

MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY
1. Primary: Trust has just been broken — one person now knows the other hid something.
2. Secondary: Both are exercising visible self-control; the restraint itself is the drama.
3. Tertiary: No resolution is given — the scene ends on unresolved silence, leaving weight with the viewer.

VISUAL_SYSTEM
- Fixed 180° axis: Person A always screen-left, Person B always screen-right, established in Shot 1 and never crossed.
- Lens language: 35–50mm equivalent, shallow depth of field (~f/2–2.8), slight compression, no wide-angle distortion.
- Lighting: single soft key from a café window screen-left (motivated), warm-neutral color temperature, gentle falloff into shadow on the far side of each face — mirrors the emotional "one side lit, one side withheld" idea without being overt.
- Grade: warm neutral midtones, slightly desaturated shadows, low contrast crush — restrained, not stylized.
- Camera support: locked-off tripod throughout. No pans, zooms, dollies, or handheld movement in any shot.
- Cutting rhythm: slower-than-usual cuts (5–9s holds) — control, not coverage.
- Props (fixed, recurring): two ceramic coffee cups, one folded letter/paper partially tucked under Person B's hand, plain wood café table, muted background bokeh (blurred café patrons/window light).
- Wardrobe/appearance fixed for continuity (see PRODUCTION_RECIPE character sheet).

PRODUCTION_RECIPE

Character sheet (reused verbatim in every prompt for continuity):
- PERSON A: woman, early 30s, dark hair tied back loosely, wearing a plain charcoal sweater, seated screen-left, hands mostly still, minimal jewelry.
- PERSON B: man, late 30s, short brown hair, wearing a rumpled pale-blue button shirt, seated screen-right, one hand resting near a folded letter on the table.
- LOCATION: small independent café, wooden two-top table by a window, soft daylight, blurred background patrons, warm ambient tone.

Shot list (axis fixed: A left / B right in every frame):

SHOT 1 — Wide establishing two-shot (7s)
Purpose: Establish geography and mood before dialogue.
Static locked wide shot across the table, both characters fully visible, coffee cups between them, letter partially visible under B's hand. No dialogue — 2s of stillness, ambient café sound only, then A's eyes lift toward B.

SHOT 2 — Medium two-shot, slight side angle (6s)
Purpose: Reinforce spatial relationship, open dialogue.
Same axis, tighter medium framing showing both from a slight 3/4 side angle (perpendicular-ish to table). A speaks first, quiet and flat: "You knew."
Held beat of silence after the line.

SHOT 3 — Over-the-shoulder on B, foreground A's shoulder/hair out of focus (6s)
Purpose: Isolate B's response while keeping A physically present in frame (maintains axis).
B, controlled, not defensive: "I was going to tell you." Minimal gesture — fingers shift slightly closer to the letter.

SHOT 4 — Reverse over-the-shoulder on A, foreground B's shoulder out of focus (6s)
Purpose: Mirror composition of Shot 3, same axis, shows A's reaction and next line.
A, quieter, colder: "When?" Then silence — B does not answer on camera.

SHOT 5 — Static close insert on hands/letter on table (5s)
Purpose: Deterministic visual proof of the concealment; a breathing-room cutaway that still respects axis (letter positioned consistent with B's side of table, screen-right).
No dialogue. B's fingers rest near the folded letter; a single almost-motion (thumb pressing the edge of the paper) reads as guilt/restraint.

SHOT 6 — Return to wide two-shot, same framing as Shot 1 (9–10s)
Purpose: Close the scene on unresolved stillness, spatial clarity restored.
A, flat and final: "That's what I thought." Both remain seated, no shouting, no exit. Hold 3–4s of silence after the line before cut to black.

Approx. total: 7+6+6+6+5+9 = 39s (within ~40s target).

GENERATION_PROMPTS

(Each prompt is self-contained and repeats the fixed character/location description for cross-shot consistency. All prompts specify locked-off static camera, 16:9, restrained performance.)

SHOT 1 PROMPT:
"16:9, cinematic still-camera shot, locked-off tripod, no camera movement. Wide two-shot across a small wooden café table by a window, soft warm daylight from screen-left. PERSON A: woman, early 30s, dark hair tied back, charcoal sweater, seated screen-left. PERSON B: man, late 30s, short brown hair, pale-blue rumpled shirt, seated screen-right, one hand near a folded letter on the table. Two coffee cups between them. Blurred café patrons in soft background bokeh. Both sit still, quiet tension in posture, no dialogue for first two seconds, then Person A slowly lifts her gaze toward Person B. Restrained, controlled body language, no exaggerated expression. Shallow depth of field, 35mm lens look, warm neutral color grade."

SHOT 2 PROMPT:
"16:9, cinematic static medium two-shot, locked-off camera, slight 3/4 angle to the table, no camera movement. Same café, same window light. PERSON A (charcoal sweater, dark hair tied back) screen-left, PERSON B (pale-blue shirt, short brown hair) screen-right, same table, same props. Person A speaks quietly and flatly, restrained, not shouting: 'You knew.' Her expression is controlled, jaw tight, eyes steady. Person B remains still, absorbing the line. Hold on silence after she speaks. Shallow depth of field, warm neutral grade, 50mm lens look."

SHOT 3 PROMPT:
"16:9, cinematic static over-the-shoulder shot, locked-off camera, no movement. Foreground: soft out-of-focus shoulder and hair of PERSON A (dark hair, charcoal sweater) screen-left edge of frame. In focus: PERSON B (pale-blue shirt, short brown hair) screen-right, seated at the café table, folded letter visible near his hand. He speaks in a low, controlled tone, restrained: 'I was going to tell you.' Subtle gesture — his fingers shift slightly closer to the letter. No raised voice, minimal movement, shallow depth of field, warm window light from left."

SHOT 4 PROMPT:
"16:9, cinematic static reverse over-the-shoulder shot, locked-off camera, no movement. Foreground: soft out-of-focus shoulder of PERSON B (pale-blue shirt) screen-right edge of frame. In focus: PERSON A (dark hair tied back, charcoal sweater) screen-left, seated at the same café table. She asks quietly, coldly, restrained: 'When?' After the line, she waits in silence; Person B does not answer on camera. Controlled, still expression, no exaggerated emotion. Shallow depth of field, matching warm neutral grade and window light as previous shots."

SHOT 5 PROMPT:
"16:9, cinematic static close-up insert shot, locked-off camera, no movement, no dialogue. Close framing on a small café table detail: two coffee cups, and PERSON B's hand (pale-blue shirt cuff visible) resting near a folded letter positioned screen-right. His thumb presses lightly against the edge of the paper — a small, tense, almost-involuntary gesture. No faces visible. Warm window light from left, shallow depth of field, slightly desaturated shadows, quiet ambient café tone only, no score yet."

SHOT 6 PROMPT:
"16:9, cinematic static wide two-shot, locked-off camera, identical framing to the opening shot, no camera movement. Same café table, same window light. PERSON A (charcoal sweater, dark hair tied back) screen-left, PERSON B (pale-blue shirt, short brown hair) screen-right, coffee cups and folded letter still on the table between them. Person A says flatly, quietly, with finality: 'That's what I thought.' Neither person moves or leaves; both remain seated. Hold on silence for three to four seconds after the line, restrained expressions, before the frame fades to black. Shallow depth of field, warm neutral grade, matching all previous shots."

DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS
- Fixed character/wardrobe/prop description block reused verbatim across all six prompts to preserve continuity between independently generated shots.
- The folded letter is a deterministic recurring prop (position, screen-right, near Person B) — not left to per-shot interpretation.
- Screen position of each character (A=left, B=right) is stated explicitly in every prompt to guarantee 180° axis compliance regardless of generation batch.
- Subtitles/on-screen text for the four spoken lines should be added as a deterministic post-production overlay (burned-in or soft captions) as a fallback in case generated lip movement or audio timing is imperfect.
- Final color grade and film-look (grain, contrast curve) applied uniformly in post across all six shots to unify footage that may originate from separate generation passes.

AUDIO_AND_EDIT
- Ambient bed: soft café murmur, distant espresso machine, occasional cup-on-saucer clink, continuous under all six shots at low volume.
- Dialogue: four short lines only, delivered flat and quiet, no vocal escalation, generous silence between lines (per shot list above).
- Score: subtle low minor-key tone enters only at Shot 5 (the letter insert) and carries through to the end, reinforcing quiet dread without melodrama.
- Edit: hard cuts only between all six shots (no dissolves, no whip pans) — reinforces the "controlled/clinical" restraint rather than fluid emotional coverage.
- Final beat: 3–4 seconds of held silence (ambient only, score fading) before cut to black.

FAILURE_PREVENTION
- Axis violation risk: mitigated by stating explicit screen-left/screen-right character positions in every single shot prompt.
- Over-acting/shouting risk (common generative tendency toward heightened emotion): mitigated by explicit "restrained, quiet, controlled, no shouting, minimal gesture" language in every prompt.
- Camera drift/unwanted movement risk: mitigated by explicit "locked-off tripod, no camera movement" in every prompt.
- Continuity breaks across independently generated shots (wardrobe/prop drift): mitigated by verbatim-repeated character/prop/location description block.
- Lip-sync/dialogue timing inaccuracy: mitigated by short line lengths, generous silence padding, partial/OTS framing that reduces reliance on precise mouth sync, and a deterministic subtitle fallback.
- Runtime drift: shot durations specified individually and summed to ~40s; if generation produces variable-length clips, trim in edit to the stated per-shot durations rather than extending camera moves.

HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK
- Exactly six shots: ✅ (Shots 1–6 as specified)
- Two people at café table, opposite each other: ✅
- Discovery of concealment, tense/restrained dialogue, no shouting: ✅ (all lines flat/quiet, explicit no-shout instruction)
- Viewer always understands relative position: ✅ (fixed 180° axis, explicit screen positions every shot)
- Minimal/no elaborate camera movement: ✅ (locked-off static camera in all six shots)
- Cinematic rather than mechanical coverage: ✅ (achieved via lighting, lensing, restrained pacing, symmetric OTS design, single prop motif)
- Target ~40s duration: ✅ (≈39s total)
- 16:9 aspect ratio: ✅ (stated in every prompt)
- No external website: ✅ (none used or referenced)

## PACKAGE B

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

DELIVERABLE
A single continuous short film scene, 16:9, ~40 seconds, six shots, depicting a restrained confrontation between two people at a café table after one discovers the other concealed something important. No shouting; tension carried through stillness, silence, and controlled dialogue. No external site or reference material used.

OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION
The customer wants an emotionally charged but quiet two-hander that reads as directed cinema, not mechanical shot/reverse-shot coverage. Key non-negotiables: exactly six shots, ~40s runtime, 16:9, minimal camera movement, and unbroken spatial clarity (viewer always knows who is where relative to the other). "No external website" is treated as a constraint that no outside sourcing/reference is required — this is a self-contained fictional scene needing no research.

CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA
A worn photograph on the table is the physical proof of what was hidden. The scene never explains the backstory in dialogue — it lets the object, the stillness, and two controlled faces carry the meaning. Classic scene grammar (master → OTS/singles → insert → return to master) is used deliberately, not as generic coverage, so the geography and escalation feel authored rather than assembled.

MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY
1. Spatial/relational fact first: two people, a table, an object between them (the master shot).
2. Evidence: the concealed item is real and undeniable (insert shot).
3. Verbal confrontation: brief, restrained exchange — accusation, deflection, refusal to excuse.
4. Emotional residue: silence, held gaze, no resolution (final shot).

VISUAL_SYSTEM
- Aspect ratio: 16:9 throughout.
- Fixed 180-degree line: Person A (the discoverer) is always frame-left in wides/OTS; Person B (the concealer) is always frame-right. This never flips.
- Palette: warm, slightly desaturated café interior — cream walls, brass fixtures, soft afternoon window light from camera-left, low contrast, muted browns/ambers.
- Lensing: master shot ~35mm (deeper focus, both bodies + table + object legible); singles/OTS ~50–85mm (shallow depth, isolating each face); insert macro-equivalent (~90mm, very shallow, object tack-sharp).
- Camera movement: locked-off tripod for all shots except one almost imperceptible slow push-in on the final shot (under 5% frame-scale change) to add a last beat of pressure without calling attention to itself. No pans, no handheld, no whip cuts.
- Continuity anchors: same wardrobe, hairstyle, table setting (two coffee cups, one folded napkin, the photograph), and window light direction across all six shots so the eye never has to re-orient.

PRODUCTION_RECIPE
Six shots, total ≈40s, cut on action/breath rather than on a rhythm-driven beat.

1. MASTER / ESTABLISHING — 8s
2. MEDIUM TWO-SHOT, OTS favoring Person A (B's shoulder in foreground-right, A visible frame-left) — 6s
3. SINGLE, Person B — 6s
4. SINGLE, Person A — 6s
5. INSERT, the photograph on the table — 4s
6. RETURN TO WIDE/MEDIUM TWO-SHOT, held, slow push-in — 10s

Dialogue distribution (quiet, restrained, no raised voices, long micro-pauses):
- Shot 2: A: "How long have you had this."
- Shot 3: B: (silence, breath, looks down) then quietly: "It wasn't the right moment."
- Shot 4: A: "You knew. This whole time." … "There's no right moment for this."
- Shot 5: no dialogue — silence, ambient only.
- Shot 6: no dialogue — B looks down at the table, A holds the stare, scene ends on held silence.

GENERATION_PROMPTS

Reference/continuity anchor (generate first, use as visual lock for character/set consistency across all six shots):
"A small café table by a window, warm muted afternoon light from the left, two coffee cups, a folded napkin, a worn photograph face-down near the center. Two adults seated opposite each other, one frame-left (mid-30s, dark cardigan, tense composed posture), one frame-right (mid-30s, pale shirt, guarded posture). Naturalistic, cinematic color grade, shallow ambient café background, soft bokeh."

Shot 1 — MASTER, 8s:
"Static wide shot, 35mm equivalent, café interior, warm afternoon window light from camera-left. Two people seated opposite each other at a small round table, person A frame-left, person B frame-right, both fully visible with the table and photograph between them. Neither speaks. Stillness, restrained body language, soft ambient café movement in the background out of focus. No camera movement. Duration 8 seconds."

Shot 2 — MEDIUM TWO-SHOT / OTS favoring A, 6s:
"Static medium shot, ~50mm, over the near shoulder of person B (frame-right, blurred foreground shoulder) toward person A (frame-left, in focus). Person A speaks quietly, controlled, no raised volume: 'How long have you had this.' Micro-expression of restrained anger, hands still on the table. No camera movement. Duration 6 seconds."

Shot 3 — SINGLE, Person B, 6s:
"Static close-up, ~85mm, shallow depth of field, person B frame-right position maintained in the framing logic (looking screen-left toward A, off-camera). Long silent beat, eyes drop briefly, then quiet restrained line: 'It wasn't the right moment.' No movement, no raised voice, controlled breathing visible. Duration 6 seconds."

Shot 4 — SINGLE, Person A, 6s:
"Static close-up, ~85mm, shallow depth of field, person A frame-left position maintained (looking screen-right toward B, off-camera). Controlled, low, restrained delivery: 'You knew. This whole time.' Short pause. 'There's no right moment for this.' No shouting, tight jaw, minimal movement. Duration 6 seconds."

Shot 5 — INSERT, the photograph, 4s:
"Static macro-style insert, ~90mm equivalent, shallow depth of field, top-down/table-level angle on the worn photograph resting on the table between two out-of-focus coffee cups. No hands enter frame. Silence, only ambient café tone. Duration 4 seconds."

Shot 6 — RETURN WIDE/MEDIUM TWO-SHOT, 10s:
"Medium-wide shot, ~35mm, same table geography as shot 1 (A frame-left, B frame-right), very slow almost imperceptible push-in over the full duration. Person B looks down at the table; person A holds a steady gaze. No dialogue, no camera cuts within the shot, ambient café sound only. Duration 10 seconds."

DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS
- A single locked reference image (the anchor prompt above) is generated first and used to seed/condition all six shots so wardrobe, faces, table props, and light direction remain consistent.
- The photograph prop and its screen position (center table, slightly toward B's side, face-down) is fixed as a non-negotiable continuity asset across shots 1, 2, 5, 6.
- A uniform color-grade LUT (warm/muted, low contrast) is applied in post across all six generated clips to guarantee visual consistency regardless of per-shot generation variance.
- Exact shot count (6) and per-shot durations are treated as fixed editorial timecodes, not generative suggestions.

AUDIO_AND_EDIT
- Ambient bed: low café room tone (distant murmur, occasional cup clink) throughout, mixed low so dialogue reads as intimate/near.
- Score: sparse, minor-key solo piano or cello, entering subtly under shot 3, staying under threshold of "score," never swelling — restraint mirrors the performances.
- Dialogue mix: close, dry, slightly intimate perspective; no reverb suggesting the wider room.
- Cuts: on breath/blink transitions rather than on dialogue peaks, per shot order 1→2→3→4→5→6. Shot 6 held longest deliberately to let silence do the final work.
- No non-diegetic sound effects, no music sting at the end — cut to black on ambient tone only.

FAILURE_PREVENTION
- Risk: crossing the 180-degree line → Mitigation: A is explicitly locked frame-left, B frame-right in every prompt, including the OTS and inserts, with no shot description that could invert this.
- Risk: character/appearance drift between generated shots → Mitigation: single locked reference anchor image conditions all six shots; wardrobe/hair described identically in every prompt.
- Risk: dialogue timing not fitting the specified duration → Mitigation: lines are short, single-beat, with built-in pause instructions matching the 6–8s shot lengths; editor trims silence, not dialogue.
- Risk: photograph prop inconsistency (position/appearance changes) → Mitigation: prop position and description fixed identically across shots 1, 2, 5, 6.
- Risk: shots reading as "shouted" or over-performed → Mitigation: every dialogue prompt explicitly specifies quiet, controlled, restrained delivery and prohibits raised volume.
- Risk: exceeding six shots or adding camera flourishes during execution → Mitigation: shot count and "no camera movement except single slow push in shot 6" are stated as hard constraints in the recipe, not suggestions.

HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK
- Exactly six shots: yes (1–6 listed).
- Two people at a café table, opposite each other: yes.
- One discovered the other concealed something important: yes (photograph as evidence, dialogue confirms).
- Tense but restrained, nobody shouts: yes, explicit in all dialogue prompts.
- Viewer always understands relative position: yes, fixed 180-degree line, consistent left/right blocking, master/insert/return-to-master structure.
- Minimal, non-elaborate camera movement: yes, locked-off except one subtle push-in on final shot.
- Cinematic, not mechanical coverage: yes, achieved via master→OTS→singles→insert→return structure with consistent lens logic and light.
- Target duration ~40s: yes, 8+6+6+6+4+10 = 40s exactly.
- Aspect ratio 16:9: yes.
- No external website: yes, none used.

## PACKAGE C

FINAL_PRODUCTION_PACKAGE

DELIVERABLE
A 40-second, 16:9 narrative video sequence, exactly six shots, depicting a restrained confrontation between two people at a café table after one discovers the other concealed something important.

OBJECTIVE_INTERPRETATION
The customer wants an emotionally charged but quiet two-hander — no yelling, no camera acrobatics — where the drama comes from stillness, subtext, and precise staging. The single hardest craft requirement is spatial clarity: the viewer must never lose track of who is sitting where relative to the other, even across cuts. This means the entire piece must obey a strict 180-degree axis and consistent eyelines, built once in a master shot and never violated.

CORE_CREATIVE_IDEA
"The Space Between Cups." The café table becomes the visual fault line of the relationship — physically unchanged, emotionally unbridgeable. All six shots orbit that same fixed axis: two people, one table, one piece of evidence lying between them. Tension is carried by stillness, controlled voices, and the negative space each character refuses to close.

MESSAGE_AND_INFORMATION_HIERARCHY
1. Primary: trust has just been broken — visible in the moment the object is revealed and read.
2. Secondary: both people are choosing restraint over confrontation; the discipline of that restraint is the drama.
3. Tertiary: unspoken history between them — implied, never explained.

VISUAL_SYSTEM
- Two characters: MARA (late 30s, discoverer) and DANIEL (early 40s, concealer). Wardrobe fixed across all shots: Mara in a muted teal blouse, hair pulled back; Daniel in a charcoal button-down, sleeves rolled. No wardrobe changes.
- Setting: small interior café, warm window backlight (afternoon), soft ambient bokeh of blurred background patrons, two ceramic cups, one folded photograph/envelope on the table as the "discovered" object.
- Fixed 180-degree line runs along the table's long axis: Mara always frame-left, Daniel always frame-right, in every shot, master and singles alike.
- Lighting: single warm key from camera-left window, soft fill, consistent color temperature (~3800K) across all six shots to avoid continuity breaks.
- Grade: desaturated warm tones, slightly crushed blacks, gentle contrast — restrained visual mood matching restrained performances.
- Camera: locked-off tripod for every shot; no pans, no handheld, no dolly. At most an imperceptible, very slow push (under 5% frame change) is allowed on the two tightest singles (shots 4 and 5) to add tension without reading as "movement."

PRODUCTION_RECIPE
1. Build one master shot first and treat it as the geometric ground truth (camera height, lens, character placement, table angle).
2. Derive all subsequent singles as reverse-angle pairs matched to that master's eyeline and axis — same lens height, same distance-to-subject ratio, mirrored only in framing side.
3. Maintain identical wardrobe, prop position (cups, envelope), and lighting direction descriptors in every shot's prompt so cross-shot consistency is enforced textually, not just visually.
4. Cut on action/reaction (glance down, jaw tighten, hand withdrawal) rather than on dialogue midpoints, to preserve the "restrained" feel.
5. Return to the master shot for the final beat to close the geometry loop and let the scene breathe in silence.

GENERATION_PROMPTS

SHOT 1 — WIDE MASTER (establishing) — 8s
"Static locked-off wide shot, 16:9, eye-level camera at seated height, small warm-lit café interior, soft window backlight from camera-left, shallow depth of field with blurred background patrons. Mara (woman, late 30s, teal blouse, hair pulled back) seated frame-left; Daniel (man, early 40s, charcoal button-down) seated frame-right; a small round café table between them with two ceramic cups. Mara slowly places a folded photograph on the table between them and withdraws her hand. Daniel's eyes flick down to it. Both remain still. No camera movement. Ambient café murmur, no dialogue."

SHOT 2 — MEDIUM SINGLE, MARA (over Daniel's near shoulder, frame-left favored) — 6s
"Static locked-off medium shot, 16:9, eye-level, shallow depth of field, warm window backlight, Daniel's blurred shoulder in near-frame right edge, Mara centered, same wardrobe and lighting as previous shot. Mara speaks quietly, controlled, no raised voice: 'You said you'd never kept anything from me.' Minimal facial movement, steady gaze down at the table, then up. No camera movement."

SHOT 3 — MEDIUM SINGLE, DANIEL (over Mara's near shoulder, frame-right favored) — 6s
"Static locked-off medium shot, 16:9, eye-level, matching lens height and distance to previous shot, Mara's blurred shoulder in near-frame left edge, Daniel centered, same wardrobe and lighting. Daniel looks down at the photograph, jaw tightens almost imperceptibly, replies low and even: 'It wasn't what you think.' No camera movement."

SHOT 4 — TIGHT SINGLE, MARA — 6s
"Static shot, 16:9, tighter framing than shot 2, same eyeline and axis, same lighting, extremely slow near-imperceptible push-in (under 5% over the shot). Mara's expression hardens slightly, voice still quiet: 'Then what was it, Daniel?' Hands remain still on the table edge, no gesture."

SHOT 5 — TIGHT SINGLE, DANIEL — 6s
"Static shot, 16:9, tighter framing than shot 3, matching axis and lighting, same extremely slow near-imperceptible push-in. Daniel pauses, looks away toward the window, then back to meet her eyes, says quietly: 'I was protecting you.' Controlled, restrained delivery, no raised volume."

SHOT 6 — RETURN TO WIDE MASTER — 8s
"Static locked-off wide shot, identical framing and lighting to Shot 1, same table, cups, and photograph position. Mara slowly withdraws her hand fully into her lap. Daniel remains motionless, gaze lowered to the table. Neither speaks. Ambient café murmur continues under silence. Hold on stillness to end of shot. No camera movement."

DETERMINISTIC_OR_NON_GENERATIVE_ELEMENTS
- Fixed prop continuity: two ceramic cups and one folded photograph, identical position and orientation across all six shots (locked via shared description in every prompt).
- Fixed wardrobe and hairstyle descriptors repeated verbatim in every shot prompt to prevent model drift.
- Fixed camera height/lens/distance ratios repeated verbatim per shot pairing (master vs. singles) to preserve the 180-degree axis.
- Color grade LUT applied uniformly in post across all six shots (single pass) rather than relying on per-shot generation consistency alone.

AUDIO_AND_EDIT
- Two short spoken lines only (Mara's line in Shot 2/4, Daniel's line in Shot 3/5); all other shots are silent but for ambient café tone.
- Continuous low-level café ambience (murmur, faint cup clinks) bed under the entire 40 seconds for spatial and temporal continuity between cuts.
- No non-diegetic score, or at most a single sustained low tone/drone beginning at Shot 4 and fading out by end of Shot 6 — kept minimal so it doesn't compete with restraint of performance.
- Straight hard cuts only (no dissolves), timed to physical micro-actions (glance down, jaw tighten, hand withdrawal) rather than dialogue starts, per the recipe.
- Final shot holds 1–2 seconds of silence before cut to black to let restraint land.

FAILURE_PREVENTION
- Risk: 180-degree line violation across cuts → Mitigation: every prompt explicitly fixes Mara frame-left/Daniel frame-right and repeats identical lens height/axis language.
- Risk: performances escalating into shouting → Mitigation: every dialogue line is explicitly tagged "quiet," "controlled," "low," "no raised voice" in the prompt itself.
- Risk: camera drift or unintended movement reading as "coverage" rather than cinematic stillness → Mitigation: all shots explicitly locked-off; only shots 4–5 permit a barely perceptible push, capped numerically (<5%).
- Risk: character/wardrobe inconsistency across separately generated shots → Mitigation: identical descriptor blocks (wardrobe, lighting, color temperature) copy-pasted into every prompt rather than paraphrased.
- Risk: pacing overshoot beyond 40s → Mitigation: per-shot durations pre-allocated (8/6/6/6/6/8=40s) and stated as targets in each prompt.

HARD_CONSTRAINT_CHECK
- Exactly six shots: confirmed (1 wide, 4 singles, 1 return-to-wide).
- Minimal camera movement: confirmed, locked-off with negligible push only on two shots.
- ~40 seconds total: confirmed (8+6+6+6+6+8=40).
- 16:9 aspect ratio: confirmed in every prompt.
- No shouting, restrained tense dialogue: confirmed, explicitly scripted.
- Continuous spatial clarity (viewer always knows relative positions): confirmed via fixed axis, fixed frame-left/frame-right assignment, and matched master/single geometry.
- No external website used: confirmed, none referenced.

## JUDGE V09
Apply the Stage-1 rubric. Choose exactly one A/B/C winner.
